1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the integration space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 78 and the convergence rate is O(635/n^3).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 78 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the integration structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 635. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
